- **boil-the-ocean**: Do the whole thing. The marginal cost of completeness is near zero with AI, so attempting the full-scope solve is now the default, not the overreach. Invoke when Ariel says "boil the ocean", "do the whole thing", "no shortcuts", "complete solve", or "Garry mode".
